Code: python
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
Inputs: {'type_approval_mode': True, 'encryption_keys': '/local/rcs/XXX/code/pytrace-collector/logs/pypibugs/tried/JRCSTU+co2mpas-ta/JRCSTU+co2mpas-ta/tests/files/keys/dice.co2mpas.keys', 'output_folder': './outputs', 'sign_key': './DICE_KEYS/sign.co2mpas.key', 'only_summary': False, 'augmented_summary': False, 'hard_validation': False, 'declaration_mode': False, 'enable_selector': False, 'encryption_keys_passwords': './DICE_KEYS/secret.passwords'}

Reason in a direct trace-first way.

A productive pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Identify the true entry path, the incoming input state, and the first statement that actually runs.
- Keep one concrete state trace: after each important step, know the current values, branch outcome, mutation, return value, and any printed fragment.
- When a helper call or loop segment is easy to lose track of, make a tiny running ledger for that same path rather than switching to a high-level explanation.
- If a doubt appears, resolve it from the traced states by continuing one more step or doing one small local recalculation.
- If a short checkpoint is needed, make it a compact summary of the current state and then return immediately to the next executed step.
- Once the output is fixed by the trace, do one brief consistency check on that same path and answer.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- turning execution simulation into algorithm discussion;
- stepping back from a concrete trace into abstract reinterpretation;
- replacing explicit values and state changes with guesses about what the code probably means.

Let's search memory more concretely. I recall a utility `sh.selector` in co2mpas used to select keys from a dictionary and format as command line. There is a function `sh.selector` in `co2mpas.utils.sh`:
```
def selector(selector, data, output_type='dict'):
    """
    Selects data based on selector.

    :param selector:
        Selector dictionary.
    :param data:
        Data dictionary.
    :param output_type:
        Output type: 'dict', 'list', 'args'
    """
    if output_type == 'dict':
        return {k: data.get(v) for k, v in selector.items()}
    elif output_type == 'list':
        return [data.get(v) for k, v in selector.items()]
    elif output_type == 'args':
        return [item for k, v in selector.items() for item in (k, data.get(v)) if data.get(v) is not None]
```

```
def selector(selector, data, output_type='dict'):
    """
    Selects values from data using selector.

    :param selector:
        A dictionary with keys the output keys and values the input keys.
    :param data:
        A dictionary.
    :param output_type:
        'dict' -> {output_key: data[input_key]}
        'list' -> [data[input_key] for input_key in selector.values()]
        'items' -> [(output_key, data[input_key]) for output_key, input_key in selector.items()]
    """
    if output_type == 'dict':
        return {k: data.get(v) for k, v in selector.items()}
    elif output_type == 'list':
        return [data.get(v) for v in selector.values()]
    elif output_type == 'items':
        return [(k, data.get(v)) for k, v in selector.items()]
```

```
def combine_dicts(*dicts, **kwargs):
    base = kwargs.get('base', {})
    for d in dicts:
        base.update(d)

def selector(selector, data, output_type='dict'):
    if output_type == 'dict':
        return {k: data.get(v) for k, v in selector.items()}
    elif output_type == 'list':
        return [data.get(v) for v in selector.values()]
    elif output_type == 'items':
        return [(k, data.get(v)) for k, v in selector.items()]
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```


Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {

    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',

    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',

    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',

}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```

    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',

}
```

```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```

```
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',

}
```

```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```

    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',

    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',

}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```


Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

```
    return res
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',

    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

```
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:

            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )

    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```

Let's search memory of co2mpas `__main__.py` from GitHub. I think I can recall the actual code around `parse_cmd_flags`:
```
def parse_cmd_flags(cmd_flags=None):
    flags = sh.combine_dicts(cmd_flags or {}, base={
        'only_summary': False,
        'hard_validation': False,
        'declaration_mode': False,
        'enable_selector': False,
        'type_approval_mode': False,
        'encryption_keys': None,
        'sign_key': None,
        'output_template': sh.NONE,
        'encryption_keys_passwords': None,
        'output_folder': './outputs',
        'augmented_summary': False
    })
    flags['declaration_mode'] |= flags['type_approval_mode']
    flags['hard_validation'] |= flags['declaration_mode']
    if flags['declaration_mode'] and not flags['type_approval_mode'] and \
            flags['enable_selector']:
        log.info(
            'Since CO2MPAS is launched in declaration mode the option '
            '--enable-selector is not used.\n'
            'If you want to use it remove -DM from the cmd.'
        )
        flags['enable_selector'] = False
    return sh.selector(_cmd_flags, flags, output_type='list')
```
And `_cmd_flags` is:
```
_cmd_flags = {
    '--only-summary': 'only_summary',
    '--hard-validation': 'hard_validation',
    '--declaration-mode': 'declaration_mode',
    '--enable-selector': 'enable_selector',
    '--type-approval-mode': 'type_approval_mode',
    '--encryption-keys': 'encryption_keys',
    '--sign-key': 'sign_key',
    '--output-template': 'output_template',
    '--encryption-keys-passwords': 'encryption_keys_passwords',
    '--output-folder': 'output_folder',
    '--augmented-summary': 'augmented_summary',
}
```
